Do you think the voices and beliefs are a direct result of your depression, or do you feel they are separate issues that just happen to coincide with your low mood?"

Do you think the voices and beliefs are a direct result of your depression, or do you feel they are separate issues that just happen to coincide with your low mood?\n\nPatient: I feel like they are directly connected to my depression, because I never had these voices or beliefs back when I was working and feeling like myself. Since the voices are always talking about the bills and me being a failure, they feel completely tied to my low mood.\n\nDoctor: You've mentioned that the voices and beliefs seem to be connected to your feelings of depression and financial stress, but can you tell me if there are any times when the content of the voices or the nature of these beliefs seems to shift or change, perhaps becoming more or less intense in relation to your mood or specific events?"

fraud,” but when I ask my wife if she hears it, she looks at me like I’m crazy because there’s nothing there. \n- S024 (Depressed_Mood): Most days I wake up with this heavy, sinking feeling in my chest, like there’s no point to anything anymore and I’ve already failed my wife and myself, and it just hangs over me from morning until I finally fall asleep. \n- S025 (Loss_of_Interest): I used to get excited about tending the garden or brewing a new batch of beer, but now I just stare at the tools or the empty carboys and feel nothing, so I end up sitting in the garage doing nothing for hours. \n- S026 (Weight_Appetite_Change): I hardly ever feel hungry anymore and usually just pick at whatever my wife puts in front of me, sometimes going most of the day without eating because my stomach feels tied in knots and food just doesn’t seem worth the effort. \n- S028 (Psychomotor_Changes): Even simple things like getting up from the couch or walking to the kitchen feel like I’m moving through mud, and my wife will ask me something and I just sit there for a long time trying to find the words because everything in my body and head feels slowed down. \n- S029 (Fatigue): No matter how much I lie in bed or nap during the day, I still feel completely drained, like my limbs are made of concrete and just taking a shower or putting on clean clothes can wipe me out for the rest of the afternoon.\n▸ Additional Diagnostic Requirements\n- delusions_and_hallucinations_occur_exclusively_during_the_major_depressive_episode: I never had these voices or ideas about the bank and bills targeting me back when I was working and feeling more like myself; they only started after months of feeling deeply down, worthless, and stuck at home with the money problems weighing on me every day.\n- functional_impairment: I’ve basically stopped handling our finances, going to community events, or even answering the phone, and most days I just sit in the garage avoiding people and letting my wife shoulder all the bills and household responsibilities because I feel too ashamed and scared to deal with anything.\n- duration: Looking back, this heavy depression and the scary messages I think I’m getting from the bank and collectors have been going on for about seven months, ever since it became clear my back injury meant I couldn’t keep up with my handyman work and the bills started Because I feel so deeply down and stuck every single day, the intensity just stays constantly heavy and never really lets up.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the voices and beliefs about being a failure and the bank and collectors targeting you would still be present if your depression were treated and you were no longer feeling down, or are they so closely tied to your mood that they would likely disappear with improvement in your depression?"

Subtypes: Intrusive Memories, Distressing Dreams, Dissociative Reactions (Flashbacks), Psychological Distress at Cues, Physiological Reactions to Cues\n[S070] Trauma_Avoidance: Persistent avoidance of stimuli associated with the traumatic event. Subtypes: Self-Blame, Blaming Others\n[S074] Persistent_Negative_Emotional_State: Persistent negative emotional state (e.g., fear, horror, anger, guilt, or shame).  Subtypes: Persistent Fear, Persistent Horror, Persistent Anger, Persistent Guilt, Persistent Shame\n[S075] Social_Detachment: Feelings of detachment or estrangement from others. Subtypes: Significant Weight Loss, Weight Gain, Decrease in Appetite, Increase in Appetite\n[S027] Sleep_Disturbance: A state where there is a significant change in the quantity or quality of sleep.
